  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现光驱动电子结构调控可为光伏和光电化学材料优化提供新途径，需深入理解NiO等透明半导体在光激发下的电子与结构响应机制。

Method: 采用Ni K-edge X射线吸收谱，在连续和脉冲紫外激发下探测未占据态密度变化，并结合第一性原理DFT+U+V计算解析光谱演变。

Result: 脉冲激发（载流子密度约10^20 cm^-3）产生约600皮秒寿命的增强杂化态，伴随晶格加热；连续激发（约10^13 cm^-3）稳定类似电子态但晶格加热可忽略，表明形成非热驱动。计算将光谱变化归因于Ni 3d-O 2p杂化增强，改变偶极允许的Ni 4p态。

Abstract: Light-driven control of electronic structure in correlated metal oxides offers new opportunities for optimizing materials used in photovoltaic and photoelectrochemical technologies. We show that photoexcitation of NiO, a prototypical transparent semiconductor and hole-transport material, across its charge-transfer gap produces a long-lived metastable state with enhanced Ni 3d-O 2p orbital hybridization. We characterize this state using Ni K-edge X-ray absorption spectroscopy, which probes how structural and electronic changes affect the unoccupied p density of states during continuous and pulsed ultraviolet excitation. Under pulsed excitation, high carrier densities of approximately 10^20 per cubic centimeter generate a state with a lifetime of approximately 600 picoseconds, in which enhanced hybridization coexists with lattice heating. By contrast, continuous ultraviolet irradiation at much lower carrier densities of approximately 10^13 per cubic centimeter stabilizes a similar electronic state with negligible lattice heating, demonstrating that its formation is not solely thermally driven. First-principles DFT+U+V calculations attribute the spectral changes to stronger Ni 3d-O 2p hybridization, which alters the unoccupied Ni 4p states probed by dipole-allowed K-edge transitions. We attribute this change to the dynamic screening of on-site electronic correlations following photoexcitation, which redistributes the charge density. Because orbital hybridization governs carrier transport and charge-transfer energetics, our results identify photoinduced screening as a mechanism for dynamically tuning correlated oxides and suggest new design principles for optoelectronic materials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高通量吸附数据集通常只报告系综平均量（如吸附量或等温线），缺乏获得这些平均值的分子构型，限制了吸附分析、空间统计和机器学习生成分子构型等应用。

Method: 作者构建AdsZeo数据集，从191种沸石拓扑生成4,775个铝取代含钠框架实现，使用巨正则蒙特卡洛模拟在298 K和0.1–100 bar的13个甲烷压力下进行，共62,075次生产模拟；除标量记录外，保存甲烷伪原子坐标、可移动Na+坐标、框架原子坐标、每帧吸附量与能量统计，并以DuckDB数据库处理发布。

Result: 数据集包含12,415,000条保存的生产帧记录和1,245,376,215条保存的粒子坐标记录，提供覆盖拓扑、铝含量与分布、钠阳离子排列、压力和甲烷负载量变化的坐标分辨吸附数据。

Conclusion: AdsZeo填补了传统吸附数据集中缺乏分子构型信息的空白，支持吸附分析、空间统计、密度估计以及带电沸石孔道中分子构型生成的机器学习模型开发。

Abstract: Zeolites are crystalline nanoporous materials widely used in adsorption, separation, and catalytic processes. Molecular simulations are commonly used to predict adsorption properties, but most high-throughput adsorption datasets report only ensemble-averaged quantities such as loadings or isotherms, rather than the molecular configurations from which these averages are obtained. Here, we present AdsZeo, a coordinate-resolved dataset of equilibrium methane adsorption configurations in aluminium-substituted, sodium-containing zeolite frameworks. The processed release contains 4,775 framework realisations derived from 191 zeolite topologies. Each framework realisation was simulated at 13 methane pressures between 0.1 and 100 bar at 298 K using grand canonical Monte Carlo simulations, giving 62,075 production simulations in total. In addition to scalar adsorption records, the dataset stores production-frame methane pseudo-atom coordinates, mobile Na$^+$ cation coordinates, framework atomic coordinates, per-frame loading and energy statistics, and simulation metadata in a processed DuckDB database. The release contains 12,415,000 saved production-frame records and 1,245,376,215 saved particle-coordinate records. AdsZeo provides coordinate-resolved adsorption data across variations in framework topology, aluminium content and distribution, sodium cation arrangement, pressure, and methane loading, enabling reuse for adsorption analysis, spatial statistics, density estimation, and machine-learning models for molecular configuration generation in charged zeolite pores.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于铁磁体的自旋霍尔纳米振荡器受限于高阈值电流密度和大外加磁场，难以满足低功耗应用需求，亟需探索兼具高效自旋动力学与能量效率的替代材料。

Result: 优化后的振荡器同时实现低电流密度（1.43×10^7 A/cm²）、小磁场（5 mT）和窄线宽（0.61 MHz），性能较铁磁对应器件提升一个数量级，归因于补偿点附近的高自旋轨道矩效率、低磁各向异性、降低的有效磁化强度及最小化非线性。

Conclusion: 稀土-过渡金属亚铁磁体被确立为下一代自旋电子器件的优势材料平台，为实现节能高性能自旋振荡器提供了新策略。

Abstract: Conventional spin Hall nano-oscillators (SHNOs) based on ferromagnets face practical limitations due to high threshold current densities and large external magnetic field requirements. Ferrimagnets provide an attractive alternative due to their unique magnetic dynamics and potential for energy-efficient spintronic devices. In this study, we report rare-earth-transition-metal (RE-TM) ferrimagnetic SHNOs utilizing Co1-xGdx alloys, in which compositional tuning enables high-performance operation near the magnetization compensation. The optimized SHNO operates at a low current density (1.43*10^7 A/cm^2), a small magnetic field (5 mT), and exhibits a narrow linewidth (0.61 MHz) simultaneously, showing an order-of-magnitude improvement over its ferromagnetic counterparts. This enhanced performance arises from high spin-orbit torque efficiency, low magnetic anisotropy, reduced effective magnetization, and minimized nonlinearity near the compensation point. These results establish RE-TM ferrimagnets as a promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices and offer new strategies for realizing energy-efficient, high-performance spintronic oscillators.

### [77] [Wrinkling in Selected Polymer Thin Films Induced by Combined Ion Beam and Humidity Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09041)

Method: 对三种聚合物薄膜进行IBS处理，随后暴露于水蒸气；利用XPS和FTIR分别表征IBS前、IBS后以及湿度暴露后的表面化学组成、结构变化和水吸收行为。

Conclusion: 起皱是由吸水溶胀受到刚性硅基底和硬质石墨化表面层的机械约束所致；pV4D4因水吸收低、溶胀响应有限，故不起皱。

*Aditya Raghavan,Yu Liu,Ian Mercer,JP Maria,Sergei Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出多任务扫描探针显微镜（multitask SPM）工作流，利用多任务高斯过程实时学习空间与跨模态关系，自主选择下一个测量位置和实验协议，在自动化大样品原子力显微镜上以AlScN成分梯度晶圆和 tapping/DART 两种模式验证，实现从空间采样主动学习扩展到测量模态的自主分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描探针显微镜已用于晶圆级表征与组合材料探索，但不同模态在采集时间和探针/样品损伤风险上差异大，穷尽式多模态空间网格测量不现实，需要高效分配测量资源。

Method: 采用闭环多任务高斯过程，同时建模空间相关性和任务间关系；通过成对初始测量建立模态关联，之后允许非重合测量点，利用多任务模型联合更新多个响应景观；算法自主决定下一测量位置和下一实验模态（如快速弱扰动的tapping或较慢的DART）。

Result: 在自动化大样品原子力显微镜上，以AlScN成分梯度晶圆为对象，结合tapping-mode和Dual AC Resonance Tracking (DART)测量，展示了多任务主动学习的可行性：成对初始测量后，非重合测量即可同步更新两种模态的响应图。

Conclusion: 该方法将扫描探针显微镜中的主动学习从单纯的空间采样扩展至模态的自主分配，为快速弱扰动成像与较慢接触、电、机电、磁或光谱测量的结合提供了基础。

Abstract: Scanning probe microscopy provides nanoscale access to structural, electrical, electromechanical, magnetic, and mechanical properties of materials. Its increasing use for wafer-scale characterization and combinatorial materials exploration creates a need to distribute measurements efficiently across large spatial domains. This is particularly important when available modalities differ in acquisition time and potential for tip and sample damage, making exhaustive multimodal mapping over spatial grids impractical. Here, we demonstrate multitask scanning probe microscopy, a live, closed-loop workflow in which a multitask Gaussian process learns spatial and cross-modal relationships and autonomously selects both the next measurement location and the next experimental protocol. The approach is implemented on an automated large-sample atomic force microscope and demonstrated on a composition-spread AlScN wafer using tapping-mode and Dual AC Resonance Tracking (DART) measurements. Paired initial measurements establish the relation between the tasks, after which noncoincident measurements are used to update both response landscapes. The resulting workflow extends active learning in scanning probe microscopy from spatial sampling to autonomous allocation of measurement modalities and provides a basis for combining rapid, weakly perturbative imaging with slower contact, electrical, electromechanical, magnetic, or spectroscopic measurements.

</details>


### [81] [All-Electron Single-Atom Reference Correction for Absolute Transition Energies in Fixed-Reference PAW-XCH Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09216)
*Yinan Wang,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 针对VASP中PAW方法计算芯损失谱绝对跃迁能的系统低估，提出基于单原子自洽计算的残余原子参考修正，使修正后谱接近实验绝对能量尺度，并阐明化学位移由超胞总能双差决定，同时发现Al/Si L2,3边近边形状强烈依赖PAW对3d类空态的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFT计算芯损失谱绝对跃迁能通常低估；VASP使用同一PAW数据集描述基态和芯激发态，芯占据变化时保留了基态原子参考，未包含对应的全电子原子参考变化，导致误差。

Method: 使用自洽单原子计算评估该全电子原子参考变化，由价态参考变化和孤立原子总能响应构造残余原子参考修正；与独立超软赝势实现对比验证K、L1、L2,3芯洞的全电子参考数值一致性；并考察PAW表示对近边谱形的影响。

Result: 修正后代表性光谱接近实验绝对能量标度；同一元素、边和芯洞方案下原子参考项在能量差中抵消，说明同元素化学位移由超胞总能双重差决定；Al/Si L2,3边近边形状对PAW中低能3d类空态表示敏感。

Conclusion: 提出的原子参考修正可提升VASP芯损失谱绝对能量精度，且化学位移可直接由总能差计算；需注意PAW投影对未占据3d态的描述以保证近边谱形准确。

Abstract: The transition energy of a core-loss spectrum comprises a transferable atomic core-reference contribution and a material-dependent response that determines chemical shifts. Density functional theory calculations commonly underestimate absolute transition energies. In VASP, the same PAW dataset is used for the ground and core-excited occupations, retaining the ground-state atomic reference when the core occupation changes and thereby omitting the associated all-electron atomic reference change. We evaluate this change using self-consistent single-atom calculations and construct a residual atomic reference correction from the valence reference change and isolated-atom total-energy response. Comparisons with an independent ultrasoft-pseudopotential implementation demonstrate numerical consistency of the all-electron reference for K, L1, and L2,3 core holes, while representative corrected spectra approach the experimental absolute energy scale. For a fixed element, edge, and core-hole scheme, the atomic reference terms cancel from energy differences, showing that same-element chemical shifts are governed by the double difference of the supercell total energy. At the Al and Si L2,3 edges, the near-edge spectral shape additionally depends strongly on the PAW representation of low-lying 3d-like unoccupied states.

</details>


### [82] [Machine-learning octet $AB$-type binary compounds across chemical space with domain knowledge of the interatomic bond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09257)
*Rohan Kumar,Mariano D. Forti,Aakash A. Naik,Luca M. Ghiringhelli,Thomas Hammerschmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过将紧束缚键模型递归求解得到的粗粒化局域电子结构特征纳入机器学习，显著提升了八电子AB型二元化合物在闪锌矿/岩盐结构稳定性（形成能差）的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构图谱和机器学习方法依赖原子特征（价电子数、电离势、原子半径），难以充分刻画4配位闪锌矿与6配位岩盐结构之间的相对稳定性，尤其是电荷转移和原子尺寸差异的调制作用。

Method: 在核岭回归或符号回归模型中显式引入键合领域知识：利用密度泛函理论双原子分子计算下折叠本征态构造成对哈密顿量，通过递归求解紧束缚键模型生成键信息递归特征，并结合序列特征选择构建集成模型。

Result: 相比先前基于相同从头计算训练数据的符号回归模型，新模型对AB化合物形成能差的预测显著改进，且增加键信息递归特征可系统提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 将局域电子结构对应的化学键信息显式编码为特征，是提高结构稳定性预测能力的有效策略，优于仅依赖原子物理特征的旧方法。

Abstract: The prediction of the structural stability of octet $AB$-type binary compounds is a classical materials informatics problem. The challenge is to capture the relative stability of 4-fold coordinated atoms in zincblende ($β$-ZnS) structure and 6-fold coordinated atoms in rocksalt (NaCl) structure, modulated by charge transfer and atomic-size differences. Previous structure maps and machine-learning approaches used atomic features such as valence-electron count, ionization potential and atomic radii, using either physical intuition or symbolic regression. Here, we demonstrate that explicitly incorporating the domain knowledge of the interatomic bonds can significantly and systematically improve the prediction of $β$-ZnS/NaCl stability. We encode this bonding information through a coarse-grained representation of the local electronic structure obtained by a recursive solution of a tight-binding bond model. The underlying pairwise Hamiltonians are taken from downfolded eigenstates of density-functional theory calculations for diatomic molecules and thereby include domain knowledge of the bond between specific $A-B$ pairs. The benefit of this description is demonstrated with an ensemble of independently trained Kernel Ridge or symbolic regression models combined with sequential feature selection. The obtained models are compared to a previous symbolic-regression model using the same set of \emph{ab initio} calculations for octet binaries as training data. We find a significant improvement in the prediction of the formation energy difference of $AB$ compounds as compared to previous works and demonstrate that an increasing amount of bond-informed recursion features improves the predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [83] [Time-resolved study of carbonization and growth of ultrathin 3C-SiC on Si(111) under ultra-high vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09431)
*Pranjali Jadhao,Mojdeh Fallahpour,Josef Polčák,Eva Kolíbalová,Michal Horák,Jan Michalička,Petr Bábor,Stanislav Voborný,Tomáš Šikola*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在UHV条件下于800 °C对Si(111)暴露乙烯，系统研究了超薄SiC层形成的时间演化过程：Si 2p与C 1s XPS表明Si逐步转化为SiC，SiC分数在120–160 min间超过元素Si并在180 min后饱和于~80–81%，残余~19–20%元素Si；AFM/SEM显示形貌从孤立岛状演变为近连续层；AES深度剖析证明C进入近表面区域而非弱吸附污染；TEM确认闪锌矿结构的3C-SiC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Si(111)上SiC超薄层早期形成过程中的化学与形貌时间演化，为3C-SiC异质外延以及SiC/Si(111)模板在III族氮化物和碳化物（如Mo2C）生长中的优化提供依据。

Method: 在UHV中对清洁Si(111)在800 °C下暴露C2H4，时间从2 min到4 h；采用原位XPS分析化学态，结合非原位AFM、SEM、AES、SIMS和TEM表征形貌、成分深度分布及晶体结构；通过Si 2p和C 1s峰分析定量SiC与元素Si比例，并关联形貌演变。

Result: SiC比例随时间增加，在120–160 min超过元素Si，180 min后饱和于80–81%，剩余约19–20%元素Si；形貌从稀疏孤立岛演变为合并近连续层；AES深度剖析确认C在近表面层中形成碳化物而非污染吸附；TEM确认形成闪锌矿结构的3C-SiC。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了Si(111)上SiC成核、合并与层生长的时分辨图像，有助于优化SiC/Si(111)模板，进而支撑III族氮化物及其他碳化物体系在硅上的外延生长。

Abstract: The early-stage formation of silicon carbide (SiC) on Si(111) by ethylene exposure under ultra-high vacuum (UHV) was investigated to resolve the time-dependent chemical and morphological evolution of an ultrathin layer. Clean Si(111) substrates were exposed to C$_2$H$_4$ at 800 °C for 2 min to 4 h, and the surfaces followed by in-situ X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) and ex-situ AFM, SEM, AES, SIMS and TEM. Si 2p and C 1s peak analysis shows the progressive conversion of elemental silicon into a carbidic Si-C phase, the SiC fraction overtaking the elemental component between 120 and 160 min and saturating near 80-81% beyond 180 min, leaving about 19-20% residual elemental silicon. Correlative SEM and AFM reveal a parallel morphological progression, from sparse isolated islands to a coalesced, near-continuous layer. AES depth profiling confirms carbon incorporated into the near-surface region rather than weakly adsorbed as contamination, assigning the islands to early SiC nuclei. TEM confirms the zinc-blende lattice and the presence of cubic silicon carbide (3C-SiC). Together, these results provide a time-resolved picture of SiC nucleation, coalescence and layer growth on Si(111), relevant to 3C-SiC heteroepitaxy, and can be utilized in the optimization of SiC/Si(111) templates for growth of III-nitride and other carbide systems on silicon (e.g. Mo$_2$C).

</details>


### [84] [A spin-bond theory unifying non-relativistic spin splitting and emergent spin-orbit textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09644)
*S. Allende,R. M. Otxoa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出自旋键理论，将交错磁体与p波磁体统一在同一代数框架下，揭示非相对论性自旋织构由酉自旋相位和厄米自旋振幅共同决定，并预言可通过非对易几何调控的新型非共面自旋织构及其谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分类将无净磁化但产生非相对论自旋劈裂的磁序分为偶宇称交错磁体和奇宇称p波磁体，但缺乏统一理解。

Method: 引入自旋键理论，将电子键分解为自旋相位（酉）和自旋振幅（厄米）两部分，分析二者对非相对论自旋织构的代数作用，并研究其非对易混合区。

Result: 酉部分产生奇宇称p波和类自旋轨道耦合织构；厄米部分产生偶宇称自旋场（包括均匀Γ劈裂和键交错磁体）；非对易区产生偶宇称横向自旋极化的非共面自旋织构，可作为自旋分辨光电子谱的直接指纹，且可通过几何调控动态调谐。

Conclusion: 该理论为合成自旋轨道耦合的微观调控提供基础，并为无场自旋量子比特等应用开辟新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic order with vanishing net magnetization can produce non-relativistic spin-split bands, broadly categorized into even-parity altermagnets and odd-parity \(p\)-wave magnets. Here, we introduce a spin-bond theory that unifies these seemingly distinct phenomena into a single algebraic framework. We demonstrate that non-relativistic spin textures are fundamentally governed by two components of the electronic bond: unitary spin phases and Hermitian spin amplitudes. The unitary sector generates odd-parity p-wave and emergent spin-orbit-like textures, while the Hermitian sector generates even-parity spin fields, including the uniform \(Γ\)-split and bond-structured altermagnetic limits. Beyond unifying known phases, our theory uncovers a mixed non-commuting regime that emerges when the unitary and Hermitian sectors fail to commute, revealing an underlying non-commuting spin-bond structure. This regime generates a non-coplanar spin texture characterized by an even-in-momentum transverse spin polarization, providing a direct spectroscopic fingerprint for spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. Furthermore, we establish that this synthetic spin-orbit coupling can be dynamically tuned by geometrically controlling the non-commutation of the bond sectors. By providing a microscopic foundation for such tuning, our theory paves the way for advanced applications, including field-free spin qubits.

</details>


### [85] [Preserved metallicity and tunable magnetism in Zr-based Janus MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09645)
*S. Özcan,B. Biel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算系统研究了Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes（M=Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti；X=F, Cl, S）的结构、电子、力学和磁性，发现其几乎全部保持金属性，并可通过表面官能团调控磁性与力学性能，部分体系呈现铁磁/反铁磁基态、半金属性及超过室温的奈尔温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Janus MXenes的化学不对称性为调控二维过渡金属碳化物性质提供了新平台，但缺乏对其金属性保持、磁性与力学响应的系统理解，尤其与常见功能化MXene金属-半导体转变行为不同，需要探明Janus构型对电子结构的实际影响。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，对Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes进行系统的结构与电子性质模拟；通过形成能判断化学稳定性，利用Born稳定性准则验证力学稳定性，并计算电子态密度、磁性基态、磁各向异性能及奈尔温度等指标，同时评估表面官能团对弹性刚度的影响。

Result: 所有ZrMCX2均具有负形成能且满足Born稳定性准则；几乎全部体系保持金属性，未出现常见的金属-半导体转变；表面功能化显著调制费米能级附近电子态密度，强烈影响磁性与力学响应。部分化合物具有铁磁或反铁磁基态，其中三种显示半金属性，部分体系具有显著磁各向异性能且奈尔温度超过室温；功能化还增强了弹性刚度。

Conclusion: 化学不对称性与表面终止基团可在保持稳定金属骨架的前提下有效调控磁性和力学性质，使Zr基Janus MXenes成为金属性及自旋相关二维材料的重要候选体系，为二维自旋电子学与磁性材料设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Janus MXenes provide a chemically asymmetric platform to tailor the properties of two-dimensional transition metal carbides. Here, we present a systematic first-principles study of the structural, electronic, mechanical, and magnetic properties of Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes (M = Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti; X = F, Cl, S). All compositions exhibit negative formation energies and satisfy the Born stability criteria, supporting the energetic and mechanical stability of the Janus structures. In contrast to the metal-tosemiconductor transitions frequently reported for functionalized MXenes, we find that the metallic character is preserved in nearly all ZrMCX2 systems, despite the strong chemical asymmetry introduced by the Janus configuration. Surface functionalization nevertheless plays a key role in modulating the electronic density near the Fermi level and strongly influences the magnetic and mechanical responses. Several compounds exhibit ferromagnetic or antiferromagnetic ground states, three display halfmetallicity, and sizable magnetic anisotropy energies are obtained, with Neel temperatures exceeding room temperature in selected cases. Functionalization also enhances the elastic stiffness of the Janus structures. These results demonstrate that chemical asymmetry and surface termination provide effective control over magnetic and mechanical properties while maintaining a robust metallic framework, identifying Zr-based Janus MXenes as promising model systems for metallic and spindependent two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [86] [Predictive Simulation of Interphases on Li Metal Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09791)
*Xinyu Li,Jingxuan Ding,Daniel C. Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Qichao Hu,Kang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了一种结合通用极化力场与机器学习力场的模拟方法，可预测锂金属表面界面相的化学组成，无需依赖试错实验，并成功复现氟化溶剂促进LiF-rich界面、碳酸酯溶剂产生有机界面的实验规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面相是先进电池中最不为人知的部分，其性质决定新电池化学能否按设计工作，但缺乏原子层面知识导致无法可靠预测新电解液体系会产生何种界面相，因此需要一种通用预测方法以替代耗时的爱迪生式电解液工程。

Method: 将可迁移的通用极化力场与通用机器学习力场相结合，对多种化学组成不同的电解液配方进行界面相化学模拟，直接捕获锂金属表面自发界面反应，从而建立电解液化学、盐浓度、分解路径与SEI性质之间的分子级关联。

Result: 成功复现氟化溶剂促进LiF-rich界面形成、传统碳酸酯电解质产生更多有机来源界面的实验观察，并证明该方法能直接捕捉这些自发界面反应。

Conclusion: 该方法为界面相的通用、高通量预测模拟开辟了道路，可作为AI驱动电解液发现的基础。

Abstract: Interphases remain the least understood components in advanced batteries. Although their properties dictate whether a new battery chemistry could perform as designed, there has never been a reliable way to predict what an interphase could arise from a new electrolyte system due to the absence of atomistic level knowledge about interphasial formation process. In this work, we attempt to develop a simulation method that can universally predict interphasial chemistries formed on Li metal surface, so that the electrolyte engineering would no longer need lengthy Edisonian approaches. By combining a transferable universal polarizable force field and a universal machine learning force field, we simulate interphasial chemistry across chemically diverse electrolyte formulations, and successfully replicate the experimental observation that fluorinated solvents promote the formation of LiF-rich interphases, whereas interphases of more organic origin arise from conventional carbonate-based electrolytes. By directly capturing these spontaneous interfacial reactions behind these interphasial chemistries, our simulations establish molecular-level relationships between electrolyte chemistry, salt concentration, decomposition pathways, and SEI properties, and opens a route toward universal and high-throughput predictive simulation of interphases that is the foundation for AI-driven electrolyte discoveries.

</details>


### [87] [Polar-vortex-driven interfacial strain coupling in PbTiO3/SrRuO3 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09815)
*S. A. Raza,V. A. Stoica,H. Zheng,H. G. Lee,A. Ross,U. Saha,L. Q. Chen,L. W. Martin,V. Gopalan,J. W. Freeland*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在(PbTiO3)16/(SrRuO3)9/(PbTiO3)16异质结中，铁电层形成约10 nm周期的极性涡旋超结构，其产生的纳米级面内应变调制可传播到SrRuO3层，并通过元素特异性共振X射线反射率探测到Sr和Ru亚晶格的界面应变调制，证明铁电与铁磁层间存在强界面应变耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化物异质结中的界面耦合是凝聚态物理的核心问题，通常源于晶格极化与应变介导的局域相互作用；理解并利用纳米尺度的界面应变耦合，有助于通过应变和极化控制实现纳米级磁调制。

Method: 在DyScO3(110)衬底上生长(PbTiO3)16/(SrRuO3)9/(PbTiO3)16异质结，通过外延应变使PbTiO3形成极性涡旋超结构，并利用元素特异性共振X射线反射率分别探测Sr和Ru亚晶格的纳米尺度应变调制。

Result: PbTiO3层中形成了约10 nm周期的极性涡旋超结构，其面内纳米应变调制可传递进入SrRuO3层，并在界面上表现出强烈的铁电/铁磁层间应变耦合。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了通过界面应变和极化控制来工程化纳米级磁调制的可行性，为氧化物异质结中多铁性耦合调控提供了新思路。

Abstract: Interfacial coupling in oxide heterostructures is a central problem in condensed-matter physics, as it typically emerges at the atomic scale through local interactions mediated by lattice polarization and strain. In this work, we investigate nanoscale polar-supertexture-driven interfacial strain coupling in (PbTiO3)16/(SrRuO3)9/(PbTiO3)16 heterostructures grown on DyScO3(110) substrates. Under appropriate epitaxial strain conditions, the PbTiO3 layers form polar vortex superstructures with a periodicity of approximately 10 nm. We demonstrate that the resulting in-plane nanoscale strain modulation propagates into the SrRuO3 layer. Using element-specific resonant X-ray reflectivity, we probe the nanoscale strain modulations of the strontium and ruthenium sublattices at the interface, revealing strong interfacial strain coupling between the ferroelectric and ferromagnetic layers. These findings provide new insights into engineering nanoscale magnetic modulations through interfacial strain and polarization control.

</details>


### [88] [Spatially resolved elastic strain and lattice rotation at threading dislocations in HgCdTe/CdZnTe epilayers by dark-field X-ray microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09841)
*C. Yildirim,A. Benhadjira,P. Ballet,T. N. Tran Caliste,J. Baruchel,C. Detlefs,D. Brellier,M. P. Kabukcuoglu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用反射几何暗场X射线显微镜，对7微米厚(111) MCT/CZT外延层实现了衬底穿透位错及其诱生岛状缺陷的单帧成像，并定量解析出单个位错周围±(4至5)×10⁻⁵的轴向应变分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从CZT衬底穿透进入MCT外延层的位错决定中波红外焦平面阵列的少子寿命和暗电流下限，但在器件级密度下，其局域应变场仅能通过形貌术获取，而形貌术混淆晶格倾斜与弹性应变，因此需要一种能直接区分并成像应变场的方法。

Method: 采用反射几何暗场X射线显微镜，利用浅布拉格角和吸收使信号层贡献主导，同时通过物镜数值孔径使层和衬底的摇摆曲线保持卷积。在摇摆曲线两侧采集弱束图像并作差，单帧即可成像相关缺陷；并使用核平均取向差(KAM)定量分析局部应变。

Result: 成像显示衬底中呈点状的穿透位错以及外延层中由它们成核的细长面内岛状特征。KAM分析将每个位错解析为约7微米（等于层厚）的特征尺寸，同时揭示轴向应变瓣大小为±(4至5)×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 该方法能在器件级位错密度下直接对单个穿透位错的应变场进行定量成像，有效区分晶格倾斜与弹性应变，为研究位错对红外探测器性能的影响提供了新的表征手段。

Abstract: Threading dislocations (TDs) propagating from a Cd$_{1-y}$Zn$_{y}$Te (CZT) substrate into a liquid-phase-epitaxy Hg$_{1-x}$Cd$_{x}$Te (MCT) epilayer set the minority-carrier lifetime and dark-current floor of mid-wave infrared focal-plane arrays, yet at device-grade densities their local strain fields have been accessible only through topography, which conflates lattice tilt and elastic strain. We apply dark-field X-ray microscopy in reflection geometry to a \SI{7}{\micro\metre}-thick (111) MCT/CZT epilayer. Shallow Bragg angle and absorption makes the signal layer dominated while the numerical aperture of the objective keeps the layer and substrate rocking curves convolved, so weak-beam images on either side of the rocking curve and their difference image the correlated defects in a single frame: dot-like substrate TDs and the elongated, in-plane island features they nucleate in the layer. Kernel average misorientation resolves each TD as a \SI{7}{\micro\metre} signature, the layer thickness, alongside axial strain lobes of $\pm(4$ to $5)\times10^{-5}$.

</details>


### [89] [Substrate-dependent thermally driven morphological evolution of Pt$_{0.9}$Ni$_{0.1}$ thin films on sapphire and langasite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09909)
*M. Awais Fiaz,M. Greenslit,R. J. Lad,Mauricio Pereira da Cunha,Luke Doucette,S. M. Hollen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了100nm厚Pt0.9Ni0.1合金薄膜在蓝宝石和langasite衬底上的热演化，发现400-600°C发生剧烈粗化转变，最终形成小平面晶体，且衬底显著影响晶体尺寸和粗糙度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解合金薄膜在高温下的热稳定性及衬底依赖的形态演化机制，为高温器件应用中薄膜材料的可靠性提供理论依据。

Method: 采用真空退火处理，结合原子力显微镜、图像分割、高度分布分析和有效粗化指标，并基于Δ⟨r²⟩进行Arrhenius分析，量化不同温度下的形态演化路径。

Result: 两种薄膜在400°C前保持致密颗粒，400-600°C经历非均匀粗化（新的大特征群体取代初始颗粒），800°C形成小平面晶体。蓝宝石上晶体面积更大（1.67×10^5 nm²），而langasite在600°C时粗糙度最大，随后部分平滑化。

Conclusion: PtNi/Zr薄膜的热稳定性由合金迁移率、界面能量和衬底依赖的形态选择共同控制，衬底差异决定了粗化产物的尺寸和形貌演变路径。

Abstract: We examine the thermal evolution of 100-nm-thick Pt$_{0.9}$Ni$_{0.1}$ alloy films on sapphire and langasite substrates with a 10-nm Zr adhesion layer. Sequential vacuum annealing from room temperature to 800 $^\circ$C produces a substrate-dependent morphological pathway that is quantified by atomic force microscopy, image segmentation, height-distribution analysis, and effective coarsening metrics. Both types of films remain densely granular up to 400 $^\circ$C, undergo a sharp coarsening transition between 400 and 600 $^\circ$C, and evolve into coalesced faceted crystallites by 800 $^\circ$C. The mean projected feature area increases from $7.84 \times 10^2$ to $1.09 \times 10^5$ nm$^2$ on langasite and from $3.32 \times 10^2$ to $1.67 \times 10^5$ nm$^2$ on sapphire. Sapphire therefore develops the larger faceted crystallites, while langasite exhibits the larger roughness maximum at 600 $^\circ$C. Area distributions reveal that the 400-600 $^\circ$C transition does not uniformly shift the initial granular population but instead replaces it with a distinct large-feature ensemble. Height and roughness measurements show a pronounced maximum at 600 $^\circ$C, followed by partial smoothing at 800 $^\circ$C, consistent with rapid coalescence followed by faceting-limited growth. An effective Arrhenius analysis based on $Δ\langle r^2\rangle$, with $\langle r^2\rangle = \langle A\rangle/π$, identifies the 400-600 $^\circ$C interval as the dominant coarsening window. These results show that the thermal stability of PtNi/Zr films on sapphire and langasite is governed by the coupled influence of alloy mobility, interfacial energetics, and substrate-dependent morphological selection.

</details>


### [90] [AES-Debye: an Accurate, Efficient, and Scalable Engine for Debye Scattering Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09916)
*Navid Panchi,Sebastian Kuckuk,Markus Wittmann,Michael Engel,Alberto Leonardi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出AES-Debye框架，通过修正bin中心和稳健累加将原子对距离聚合法PDF，避免离散化和混叠伪影，并在CPU/GPU上高效并行，实现90 million原子体系的高精度总散射计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总散射模型对纳米材料结构表征至关重要，Debye散射方程虽严谨但计算昂贵，而现有加速方法（如分箱距离分布或网格FFT）会引入离散化与混叠伪影，损害漫散射精度。

Method: AES-Debye采用精度保持的DSE框架，将原子对距离聚合为PDF，使用修正的bin中心和数值稳健累加抑制离散化与求和误差，并设计数据局部性感知的并行架构，支持CPU和GPU高效执行。

Result: 在分布式内存CPU平台上，对包含9000万原子（(0.1 μm)^3）的体系，在数分钟内完成高分辨率总散射谱计算，展现出强可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法将精确弹性总散射计算扩展至大尺度复杂体系，同时提供高分辨率PDF，便于后续结构分析。

Abstract: Total scattering models are essential for characterizing the structure and disorder of nanoscale materials. The Debye scattering equation (DSE) provides a rigorous route to elastic total scattering, but its direct evaluation is computationally demanding because pairwise contributions must be accumulated at every scattering vector, whereas common acceleration strategies based on binned pair-distance distributions or gridded fast Fourier transforms can introduce discretization and aliasing artifacts that compromise diffuse-scattering accuracy. Here, we present AES-Debye, an accuracy-preserving DSE framework that aggregates pair distances into a pair distribution function (PDF) using corrected bin centers and numerically robust accumulation to suppress discretization and summation errors. A data-locality-aware parallel design enables efficient execution on CPUs and GPUs. We demonstrate strong scalability by computing a high-resolution total scattering profile for a system of 90 million atoms, $(0.1,μ\mathrm{m})^{3}$, in minutes on a distributed-memory CPU platform. These capabilities extend accurate elastic total scattering calculations to large, complex systems while simultaneously providing high-resolution PDFs for downstream structural analysis.

</details>

### [91] [Nonadiabatic Molecular Dynamics on Real-time Excited-State Surfaces via Machine Learning Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08095)
*Changwei Zhang,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Guo Tao,Yingzhou Li,Zhenggang Lan,Oleg V. Prezhdo,Xin-Gao Gong,Weibin Chu,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 N²AMD 的机器学习框架，通过等变神经网络预测体系哈密顿量，实现了固体材料中电子-核非绝热动力学的实时模拟，能以混合泛函精度计算激发态能量、力和非绝热耦合矢量，成本远低于从头算方法，并通过 MoS₂/WS₂ 异质结、铁电开关和 TiO₂ 极化子形成等案例展示了其能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电子和原子核的耦合非平衡动力学是化学、物理和材料科学的核心挑战，但缺乏一种通用、准确且高效的方法来刻画完整激发态景观（势能面、力和多电子态的非绝热耦合）。机器学习虽已用于基态和分子激发态，但缺乏统一框架来解决凝聚态体系的完整多态问题，且传统 NAMD 无法达到混合泛函精度。

Method: 提出 on-the-fly N²AMD（Neural network NAMD）框架，利用等变神经网络（equivariant neural network）直接预测体系的哈密顿量，从而高效获得激发态能量、原子受力和非绝热耦合矢量，并实现从头算成本的一小部分即可完成实时非绝热动力学模拟，且支持混合泛函级别的精度。

Result: 在三个典型体系中验证了性能：纠正 MoS₂/WS₂ 异质结中传统方法载流子动力学的数量级误差；实现了此前无法模拟的光致铁电开关；在混合泛函水平上捕捉 TiO₂ 中实时极化子形成过程。

Conclusion: N²AMD 突破了平衡态理论的局限，为远离平衡态材料的预测性第一性原理设计建立了新范式，使固体中 on-the-fly 非绝热分子动力学模拟成为现实，并显著拓展了可访问的精度与体系范围。

Abstract: Simulating the coupled, nonequilibrium dynamics of electrons and nuclei is a central challenge in chemistry, physics, and materials science, governing phenomena from photocatalysis to quantum information. The primary bottleneck has been the lack of a general, accurate, and efficient method for modeling the complete excited-state landscape: the potential energy surfaces, forces, and non-adiabatic couplings for multiple electronic states. While machine learning has revolutionized ground-state simulations and shown promise for excited states in molecules, a unified framework that solves the complete multi-state problem for general condensed matter systems has remained elusive. Here we introduce on-the-fly N${^2}$AMD (Neural network NAMD), a machine learning framework that makes on-the-fly NAMD in solids a reality. By employing an equivariant neural network to predict the system Hamiltonian, the framework delivers excited-state energies, forces, and non-adiabatic coupling vectors at a fraction of the cost of ab initio calculations. Crucially, it allows simulations with hybrid functional accuracy, a level of approach previously inaccessible for NAMD. We showcase its capabilities with three topical examples: correcting order-of-magnitude errors in carrier dynamics predicted by conventional procedure in a MoS$_2$/WS$_2$ heterostructure, simulating previously inaccessible photoinduced ferroelectric switching, and capturing real-time polaron formation in TiO$_2$ at the hybrid-functional level. On-the-fly N${^2}$AMD moves beyond the limitations of equilibrium theory, establishing a new paradigm for the predictive, first-principles design of materials operating far from equilibrium.

</details>


### [92] [Coordinate-Residual Physics-Driven Neural Network for Electromagnetic Inverse Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09382)
*Yutong Du,Zicheng Liu,Bo Qi,Yali Zong,Peixian Han*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种坐标残差物理驱动神经网络（CRPDNN），用于三维电磁逆散射成像，直接重建对比度分布，无需预重建区域选择，在无噪声合成数据上平均相对误差2.10%，较CSI和L2/3-FBE-WCIE分别提升精度并加速5.5倍和12.1倍，且抗噪性能良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁逆散射是非线性且不适定问题，三维成像尤其困难，现有加速物理驱动神经网络依赖预重建区域选择，区域不准确时不稳定。需要一种无需预重建、稳定且计算高效的直接重建方法。

Method: CRPDNN利用归一化空间坐标作为输入，通过残差卷积网络直接预测未知对比度分布，完全省略预重建步骤。网络由物理驱动训练，减少对标注数据的依赖，提升稳定性和效率。

Result: 无噪声三维合成实验中，CRPDNN平均相对误差2.10%，优于CSI（7.97%）和L2/3-FBE-WCIE（3.99%），并分别获得约5.5倍和12.1倍加速。二维对比验证其稳定性与效率；噪声测量下保持可靠重建；三维Fresnel实验展示实际成像潜力。

Conclusion: CRPDNN是一种稳定、高效且抗噪的3D电磁逆散射求解器，免除了预重建区域选择的脆弱性，在精度和速度上均优于基线，适合实际成像应用。

Abstract: Electromagnetic inverse scattering is a nonlinear and ill-posed problem, where accurate reconstruction is challenging due to measurement limitations, noise, and high computational costs, especially for 3-D imaging. Although physics-driven neural networks (PDNNs) reduce the dependence on labeled training data, existing accelerated PDNN frameworks often rely on preliminary reconstruction-based region selection, which may introduce instability when the selected region is inaccurate. In this paper, a coordinate-residual physics-driven neural network (CRPDNN) is proposed for 3-D electromagnetic inverse scattering. The proposed solver directly reconstructs the unknown contrast distribution using normalized spatial coordinates and a residual convolutional network, without requiring a preliminary reconstruction. For the reported noise-free 3-D synthetic cases, CRPDNN achieves an average relative error of 2.10\%, compared with 7.97\% for CSI and 3.99\% for $L_{2/3}$-FBE-WCIE, while providing approximately 5.5- and 12.1-fold speedups over the two baselines, respectively. Supplementary 2-D comparisons further confirm its stability and computational efficiency relative to existing PDNN frameworks. CRPDNN also maintains reliable reconstruction performance under noisy measurements, and the 3-D Fresnel experiments further indicate its potential for practical imaging applications. The related code is available at https://github.com/Physics-driven-methods.

</details>


### [93] [Autonomous Lindblad Realizability of Nonunitary Linear Dynamics with a Carleman Lattice Boltzmann Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09808)
*Muhammad Idrees Khan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明有限线性非酉动力学算子可被自治GKSL开放量子系统实现，当且仅当该算子可逆且幂有界；构造显式且无需后选择，并以D2Q9格子玻尔兹曼时间步的Carleman端点为实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carleman lifting将非线性多项式动力学转化为有限线性系统，但截断结果通常非酉，不能直接对应物理量子演化。作者希望建立一般判据，判断有限非酉动力学能否作为真实开放量子系统动力学实现。

Method: 通过充要条件刻画GKSL可实现性（可逆且幂有界），给出显式GKSL生成元构造：在真空相干性上编码，重复时间步中仅需一次编码和一次解码，无需端点后选择。并将该构造应用于完整D2Q9多松弛时间LB时间步，将碰撞与周期性流编译为单个Carleman端点。

Result: 证明了有限线性端点存在自治GKSL实现的充要条件；对D2Q9 LB的Carleman端点，所得GKSL演化在多个时间步上精确再现经典Carleman轨迹，与真实非线性LB动力学的差异仅为预期的Carleman截断误差。

Conclusion: 建立了有限非酉动力学自治开放量子实现的通用判据，Carleman-LB动力学作为具体示例，为量子模拟非线性动力学提供了新途径。

Abstract: Carleman lifting converts nonlinear polynomial dynamics into finite linear systems, but the resulting truncations are generally nonunitary and need not correspond to physical quantum evolution. We prove that a finite linear endpoint admits an autonomous Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) realization on vacuum coherences if and only if it is invertible and power bounded. The construction is explicit and realizes the nonunitary map directly as open-system dynamics, with no endpoint postselection and with one encoding and one decoding over repeated timesteps. We apply the result to the complete D2Q9 multiple-relaxation-time lattice Boltzmann (LB) timestep by compiling collision and periodic streaming into a single Carleman endpoint. The resulting GKSL evolution reproduces the classical Carleman trajectory over multiple timesteps, while the remaining discrepancy from nonlinear LB dynamics is the expected Carleman truncation error. The result establishes a general criterion for autonomous open-quantum realization of finite nonunitary dynamics, with Carleman--LB dynamics as a concrete example.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Quantum geometric localization length and localization criticality in an ideally flat Chern band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08042)
*Xu-Cheng Wang,Yang Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在孤立且理想的平坦Chern能带中，局域长度由量子几何决定；通过调谐量子度规，观察到从狄拉克不动点控制的普适区到临界指数连续变化的非普适区的交叉，并在普适区发现局域长度对量子几何长度的线性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平坦Chern能带中的局域化行为，并解决整数量子霍尔转变临界性的长期争议，尤其是从量子几何角度提供新视角。

Method: 在最大局域化混合Wannier基中应用转移矩阵计算，该基的空间延展由量子几何长度精确表征，并通过调谐Chern带的量子度规来研究局域化转变及其临界标度。

Result: 观察到从狄拉克不动点控制的普适区到非普适区的交叉；在普适区内，局域化长度随量子几何长度呈显著的线性依赖，支持其量子几何本质。

Conclusion: 这些发现为量子霍尔体系（如扭曲莫尔超晶格）中的局域化提供了新的量子几何视角，并为整数量子霍尔转变的临界性争议提供了新启示。

Abstract: We propose that the localization length in an isolated, ideally flat Chern band is set by quantum geometry. We explore the corresponding localization transition and its critical scaling by applying transfer matrix calculations in the maximally localized hybrid Wannier basis, whose spatial spread is exactly characterized by a quantum geometric length. Remarkably, upon tuning the quantum metric of the Chern band, we observe a crossover from a universal regime controlled by the Dirac fixed point to a non-universal regime with continuously varying critical exponents. Within the universal regime, the localization length exhibits a pronounced linear dependence on the quantum geometric length, supporting its quantum geometric nature. These findings provide a novel quantum geometric perspective on the localization in quantum Hall systems such as twisted moiré superlattices, and shed new light on the long-standing controversy over the criticality of the integer quantum Hall transition.

</details>


### [95] [Engineering correlated phases through manipulation of Van Hove singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07714)
*Thomas P. Sheerin,Maria Ramirez,Chris A. Hooley,Luke C. Rhodes*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过调控平方晶格Hubbard模型中范霍夫奇点的发散强度（从对数型到幂律型再到分裂型），系统显著影响相关电子体系的关联有序态；铁磁态对掺杂极其敏感，易随电子或空穴掺杂转变为不同有序态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制关联电子体系的有序相是量子材料设计的核心挑战，范霍夫奇点提供了一条直接调控路径，但不同发散函数形式对最终有序相的影响尚未完全清楚。

Method: 采用热点parquet重整化群和截断单位函数重整化群两种方法，系统研究具有费米能级处或附近范霍夫奇点的二维平方晶格Hubbard模型；通过单一跃迁参数t3调节范霍夫奇点从对数发散、幂律发散到奇点分裂，并考察掺杂效应。

Result: 范霍夫奇点的具体发散形式强烈影响预测的有序态；在范霍夫填充下出现的铁磁态对费米能级微小移动极不稳定，电子掺杂和空穴掺杂往往导致截然不同的有序态。

Conclusion: 调控范霍夫奇点性质是控制关联材料有序态的关键手段，研究结果为在新型体系中工程设计所需量子相提供了明确的规则。

Abstract: Controlling the ordered phases of correlated electron systems remains a central challenge in quantum materials design. Divergences in the electronic density of states, known as Van Hove singularities (VHSs), are one obvious route to such control. It is clear from recent work that the exact functional form of these divergences can profoundly affect which phases are realized; a full picture, however, remains elusive. In this work, we use both the hot-spot parquet renormalization group and the truncated-unity functional renormalization group to theoretically study the emergent correlated states of a two-dimensional square-lattice Hubbard model with VHSs at or near the Fermi level. By varying a single hopping parameter, $t_3$, we are able to change the strength of the VHS divergence in the density of states from logarithmic (for $t_3 < t_{3c}$) to power-law (for $t_3 = t_{3c}$). Further increase of $t_3$ ($t_3 > t_{3c}$) causes each original Van Hove point to split into two, both of the conventional logarithmic type. We show that which of these regimes we are in strongly influences the predicted ordered states. We also study the dependence on doping, and find that the ferromagnetic state that occurs at Van Hove filling in these models is unstable to very small shifts in the Fermi level, often giving way to distinct ordered states depending on whether the model is electron- or hole-doped. These results highlight the importance of tuning VHS properties to control ordered states in correlated materials, and offer design rules to engineer these phases in novel systems.

</details>


### [96] [Metallic Gross-Neveu criticality and superconductivity on the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ SLAC fermion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07990)
*Feng-Yu Zhang,Yin-Kai Yu,Zi-Xiang Li,Shuai Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个SO(3)对称的双层SLAC费米子模型，通过可调层间相互作用实现超越传统Gross-Neveu-Yukawa（GNY）范式的Dirac临界性。量子蒙特卡洛模拟表明，系统随相互作用增强先经历连续相变进入SO(3)对称性破缺的有序相，该相变属于Gross-Neveu-SO(3)普适类（N=6），并精确提取了临界指数；随后在更强耦合下进入层间SO(3)对称超导态，该相变为量子涨落驱动的弱一级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理中，超越传统GNY范式的Dirac临界点研究是重要前沿。标准GNY模型中，序参量通常使所有Dirac费米子获得能隙，而此处关注仅gap部分巡游费米子的新临界行为，需要探索新的普适类和非传统量子相变。

Method: 构建SO(3)对称的双层SLAC费米子模型，引入可调层间相互作用；采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）模拟，分析量子相图、相变性质，并通过有限规模标度精确提取临界指数，判定相变连续性及普适类。

Result: 系统呈现两个不同相变：第一个是DSM到SO(3)破缺有序相的连续相变，因仅gap部分费米子而偏离标准GNY，确认为Gross-Neveu-SO(3)普适类，对应N=6个不可约Dirac锥；第二个是进入SO(3)对称层间超导态的相变，数值证据表明其为量子涨落驱动的一级相变。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了对超越标准GNY范式的Dirac临界性的理解，并提供了实现具有SO(3)对称性的超导态的新平台，对凝聚态量子相变和非常规超导研究具有重要启示。

Abstract: The realization of Dirac criticality beyond the conventional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) paradigm has become a major frontier in condensed matter physics. In this work, we introduce an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric bilayer SLAC fermion model with tunable inter-layer interactions that exhibits a rich quantum phase diagram. As the interaction strength increases, the system undergoes two distinct phase transitions. The primary transition is a continuous boundary separating a Dirac semimetal (DSM) from an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-broken ordered phase. Crucially, this transition evades the standard GNY universality class because the emergent order only gaps out a subset of the itinerant fermions. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations, we establish that this transition belongs to the Gross-Neveu-$\mathrm{SO}(3)$ universality class with $N=6$ irreducible Dirac cones and precisely extract the corresponding critical exponents. At stronger couplings, a second transition drives the system into an inter-layer $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric superconducting (SC) state. We provide strong numerical evidence that this transition is a quantum-fluctuation-driven first-order transition. Our study provides new insights into the exploration of Dirac criticality beyond the standard GNY universality class, and also offers a novel platform to realize superconductivity featuring $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ symmetry.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨光谱学已能直接探测关联量子材料中动量分辨的非平衡动力学，但实验观测显示出强动量依赖的电子和集体激发响应。现有非平衡理论方法难以同时处理强局域电子关联和非局域集体涨落，导致对这些动量分辨实验结果的微观解释缺失。

Method: 采用最近发展的实时多体理论框架，在保持强局域关联与非局域集体涨落一致耦合的前提下，对光激发后的关联电子系统进行实时模拟，并追踪动量分辨的电子分布、自旋涨落谱及局域自旋响应等可观测量。

Result: 预测了瞬态节点-反节点电子加热各向异性，解释了时间分辨光电子发射和拉曼实验中的动量依赖响应；识别出导致反节点隙内态的非热谱权重转移。发现反铁磁涨落被强烈非热激发到远高于电子温度的水平但关联长度保持不变，随后通过动量空间磁振子级联向低动量模式弛豫。局域自旋响应的实时演化提供了局域磁矩形成及其光致熔化的可探测特征。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了光致超快电子与磁动力学的统一微观图像，为解释动量分辨泵浦-探测实验提供了理论基础，并展示了实时多体框架在关联量子材料非平衡研究中的强大能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in time-resolved spectroscopies have enabled direct access to the momentum-selective nonequilibrium dynamics of correlated quantum materials, revealing a strongly momentum-dependent response of electrons and collective excitations. Interpreting these observations requires a real-time theoretical framework that consistently captures the interplay between strong local electronic correlations and nonlocal collective fluctuations, a capability that remains beyond state-of-the-art nonequilibrium approaches. Using a recently developed real-time many-body framework, we resolve the momentum-selective ultrafast dynamics of a photoexcited correlated electron system. We predict a transient nodal-antinodal anisotropy in electronic heating, providing a microscopic explanation for the momentum-dependent response debated in time-resolved photoemission and Raman experiments, and identify the nonthermal spectral-weight transfer responsible for the transient antinodal in-gap states observed in ultrafast photoemission. We further uncover a momentum-selective magnetic response, in which antiferromagnetic fluctuations undergo a strongly nonthermal, quench-like excitation far above the electronic temperature while preserving their correlation length, before relaxing through a momentum-space magnon cascade toward lower-momentum modes. Finally, by tracking the real-time local spin susceptibility, we identify a dynamical, experimentally accessible signature of local-moment formation and its photoinduced melting. Our results establish a unified microscopic picture of ultrafast electronic and magnetic dynamics, providing a framework for interpreting momentum-resolved pump-probe experiments.

</details>

Abstract: The recent experimental realization of the half-filled three-color Fermi-Hubbard model on a square optical lattice provides a novel platform for exploring exotic states of matter beyond conventional SU(2) systems. In this Letter, we investigate the three-color Fermi-Hubbard model with color-dependent attractive interactions using determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We find that, at quantum degenerate temperatures, a lattice Fermi supersolid state emerges from the interplay between a moderately-to-strongly interacting two-color subsystem and a weakly coupled third-color environment. This supersolid state, characterized by the coexistence of charge-density-wave and color-superfluid orders, is highly promising for experimental detection with current techniques. Our results demonstrate that the attractive three-color Fermi-Hubbard model on a square optical lattice offers an experimentally accessible system for exploring the supersolidity of ultracold lattice fermions, requiring only a simple lattice geometry and easily tunable onsite interactions.

</details>


### [99] [Plasmon modes in quadratic and cubic nodal line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08185)
*Wei Li,Jing-Rong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在随机相位近似下研究三维二次与三次 nodal line semimetals 的等离激元模式，发现其频率随载流子密度的标度不同：二次色散为 ω_p~n^{1/2}，三次色散在大掺杂下为 ω_p~n^{2/3}、小掺杂下交叉到 ω_p~n^{3/4}；但 n^{3/4} 因态密度发散导致 r_s>1 而不定量可靠，可观测窗口仅约 10^18≲n≲10^19 cm^{-3}。两种体系在薄环极限下均存在 Ω_p^z/Ω_p^⊥→√2 的各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性 nodal line semimetals 的等离激元已被充分研究，但高阶色散（二次、三次）NLSMs 的集体激发行为仍不清楚，需要理解其等离激元模式及可区分的实验特征。

Method: 在随机相位近似（RPA）框架下推导单圈极化率，并从完整三维 Lindhard 积分数值计算其系数；通过态密度幂律、带内 Drude 响应、相空间幂次计数以及薄环极限分析等离激元色散和标度行为。

Result: 二次 NLSMs 满足 ω_p~n^{1/2}，三次 NLSMs 在大掺杂时满足 ω_p~n^{2/3}、小掺杂时交叉到 ω_p~n^{3/4}；n^{3/4} 不可靠，因为发散态密度使 r_s>1 早在 n~10^18 cm^{-3} 出现。长波极限下带内极化主导等离激元频率，带间贡献在 Ω≪μ 时低两到三个数量级；两类体系在薄环极限下都有 Ω_p^z/Ω_p^⊥→√2 的普适各向异性。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶色散 NLSMs 的集体动力学提供了理论框架，指出密度标度指数是区分二次与三次色散的关键实验特征，且这些各向异性与标度律可通过 HREELS 探测验证。

Abstract: Nodal line semimetals (NLSMs) host distinctive topological properties and unconventional collective excitations. While plasmons in linear NLSMs are well studied, those of higher-order dispersive NLSMs remain poorly understood. We investigate plasmon modes in 3D quadratic and cubic NLSMs within the random phase approximation (RPA), valid for $r_s\ll1$ but breaking down in the small-doping cubic regime where the density of states diverges. Deriving the one-loop polarizability and evaluating its coefficients numerically from the full 3D Lindhard integral, we find distinct carrier-density scaling laws: quadratic NLSMs exhibit $ω_p\sim n^{1/2}$, while cubic NLSMs show $ω_p\sim n^{2/3}$ at large doping crossing over to $ω_p\sim n^{3/4}$ at small doping. The $n^{3/4}$ law is not quantitatively reliable: the diverging density of states drives $r_s>1$ already at $n\sim10^{18}$ cm$^{-3}$, limiting the observable window to at most $10^{18}\lesssim n\lesssim10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$; below this, beyond-RPA correlations are essential. These scalings originate from the power-law density of states and intraband (Drude) response, and are analogous in phase-space power counting to bilayer and trilayer graphene, respectively. The long-wavelength intraband polarization dominates the plasmon frequency, while interband contributions are subleading (two to three orders of magnitude smaller for $Ω\llμ$). Both systems share a plasmon anisotropy $Ω_p^z/Ω_p^\perp\to\sqrt{2}$ in the thin-ring limit, a generic consequence of the torus Fermi-surface geometry. This $\sqrt{2}$ doublet, though observable by HREELS, cannot distinguish quadratic from cubic dispersion; the density scaling exponent is the true distinguishing signature. Our results provide a framework for the collective dynamics of higher-order dispersive NLSMs and suggest experimentally testable signatures accessible by HREELS.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum Formulation of Chiral Vortical Effect in Weyl Semi-metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08269)
*B. Q. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出旋转外尔费米子手征涡旋效应（CVE）的全量子描述，通过精确求解自旋波函数演化，检验半经典理论与分布假设，发现CVE本质上是非平衡现象，并阐明半经典结果成立的严格条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVE理论基于半经典框架，缺少从微观波函数出发的定量验证；需发展全量子理论以检验半经典理论和假定的分布函数，并澄清CVE是否可归为热平衡输运。

Method: 求解微观自旋波函数的精确时间演化，构建CVE的全量子公式；分析不同参考系下的分布函数f_CVE，与费米分布f_F对比，并系统考察旋转频率ω、化学势μ、体系尺寸R及各向同性等参数条件。

Result: 显示f_CVE对应无基态的Floquet谱，与热平衡费米分布定性不同，证明CVE为非平衡现象；仅当慢旋转(ωR/v_F≪1)、高化学势(μ/(ℏv_F R)≫1)和各向同性三个条件同时满足时，f_F才近似成立并恢复已知半经典结果；否则出现“空态”、偏离j_CVE∼μ²的标度律以及与费米速度v_F无关的电荷泵浦等量子效应。

Conclusion: 全量子理论为CVE提供更基础且自洽的描述，揭示其非平衡本质，并基于半金属体系给出更易实验探测的预言，拓展了对手征输运现象的理解。

Abstract: The chiral vortical effect (CVE) is the generation of an axial current in a rotating Weyl fermion; its description is presently based on semiclassical frameworks. In this work, we develop a fully quantum formulation for CVE, solving the exact evolution of microscopic spinful wavefunctions, which enables a bottom-up quantitative test of semi-classical theories and postulated distributions $f_{\text{CVE}}$ in different reference frames. Notably, it shows that $f_{\text{CVE}}$ is over a ground-state-free Floquet spectrum, qualitatively distinct from a thermal equilibrium distribution (i.e., fermi form $f_F$), underscoring CVE as a non-equilibrium phenomenon, distinguished from other chiral transports. The $f_F$ only approximately holds when three conditions are simultaneously fulfilled: (1) slow rotation $ωR/v_F\ll 1$, (2) high chemical potential $μ/(\hbar v_F R)\gg 1$, (3) isotropic symmetry, where $R$ is the size, $v_F$ is fermi velocity. In these conditions, the theory recovers established semiclassical results, including the current-response coefficients and the magnetization contribution; otherwise, it uncovers quantum phenomena such as ``void states", deviation from the semiclassical formula $j_{\text{CVE}} \sim μ^2$, a $v_F$-independent charge pumping. The theory is based on semimetals, providing more experimentally accessible detection than fundamental Weyl particles.

</details>


### [101] [Elementary magnons and interacting multi-magnon quasiparticles in the effective spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ kagome-staircase magnet Co$_{3}$V$_{2}$O$_{8}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08587)
*Yuan Xiao,Jiajun Mo,Zhenmeng Jiang,Haoyang Leng,Otkur Omar,Yanjun Li,Fengyi Song,Jianjun Ying,Shang Gao*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 得到了强各向异性交换的有效哈密顿量，定量重现单磁子谱；高能区观察到尖锐分支，其磁场斜率是单磁子模式的2至4倍，且存在不同磁子数分支间的反交叉；这些特征与计算的多磁子连续谱不符，揭示其属于相互作用的准粒子。

Conclusion: 强交换各向异性能够稳定多磁子准粒子，使其表现为尖锐、偏振依赖且场斜率增强的激发，为研究各向异性磁体中的相互作用多磁子物理提供了重要基准。

Abstract: The excitation spectrum of an anisotropic magnet provides a direct link between its microscopic Hamiltonian and interaction-driven quasiparticles. Here we use high-resolution time-domain terahertz spectroscopy to map the magnetic excitations of the three-dimensional kagome-staircase compound Co$_{3}$V$_{2}$O$_{8}$ as functions of temperature and magnetic field. At low energies, polarization-resolved spectra identify magnetic-dipole-active one-magnon modes and track their evolution across the ferromagnetic and spin-density-wave phases. Combining their field dependence with previously reported inelastic-neutron-scattering dispersions, we determine an effective spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ Hamiltonian with strongly anisotropic exchange that quantitatively reproduces the one-magnon spectrum. This model provides a noninteracting benchmark for the high-energy response, where we observe sharp branches with field slopes that are two to four times those of the one-magnon modes, together with anticrossings between branches of different magnon numbers. Their sharpness, polarization dependence, and departure from the calculated multi-magnon continua identify them as interacting multi-magnon quasiparticles that can be stabilized by strong exchange anisotropy.

</details>


### [102] [Nonequilibrium dynamics of doped Chern ferromagnets: a case study for false vacuum decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08937)
*Kilian Kuhlbrodt,Olivier Huber,Oliver Breach,Weijie Li,Xiaodong Xu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Martin Kroner,Siddharth A. Parameswaran,Atac Imamoglu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在莫尔量子材料中，通过光学泵浦在亚稳态铁磁体中制备真真空泡，发现其塌缩或膨胀取决于初始尺寸，且在整数和分数量子霍尔填充附近观测到自旋极化衰减时间延长两个数量级。

Method: 利用圆偏振 Laguerre-Gauss 光束进行光学泵浦，在亚稳态中确定性制备真真空泡，通过调节泵浦功率控制初始泡尺寸，并测量不同填充因子下自旋极化衰减动力学。

Result: 气泡的塌缩或膨胀受畴壁与体动力学竞争控制；当外磁场与铁磁矫顽场相当时，在整数和分数量子霍尔态的相称填充处，自旋极化衰减时间延长达两个数量级，且该动力学对填充因子的敏感度远超标准输运或光学测量。

Conclusion: 实验揭示了铁磁畴的非平衡动力学对 Chern 绝缘态附近填充因子的极高敏感性，为理解亚稳态衰变和耗散态制备提供了新途径，并可能成为探测奇特量子态的更灵敏手段。

Abstract: Even though metastable false vacuum decay is ubiquitous in physics, its underlying dynamics are still not well understood. Dissipative state preparation in moiré quantum materials provides an exceptional setting for exploring this physics since it allows the possibility of generating exotic quantum states that are not the ground state of the system Hamiltonian. Motivated by recent experiments demonstrating steady-state optical orientation of the spin-valley degree of freedom of holes, here we investigate dynamics of itinerant and Chern ferromagnets in the presence of an opposing magnetic field. Optical pumping using a circularly polarized Laguerre-Gauss beam allows us to deterministically prepare a true vacuum bubble embedded inside a metastable state. Depending on its initial size controlled by the pump power, we observe that the bubble collapses or expands due to an interplay between domain wall and bulk dynamics. For external magnetic fields comparable to the coercive field of ferromagnetism, we observe up to two-orders-of-magnitude prolongation of the spin polarization decay time at commensurate fillings corresponding to integer and fractional Chern insulator states. Our experiments reveal that the nonequilibrium dynamics of the ferromagnetic domains is substantially more sensitive to the precise filling factor around Chern insulator states than standard transport or optical measurements.

</details>


### [103] [Geometrical approach and topological electron density in the $p_x + ip_y$ superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09415)
*Karyn Le Hur,Kabir Narayanan,Óscar Mañas Chambert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文分析了方格上 $p_x + ip_y$ 超导体的几何与物理性质，重点研究半填充时的拓扑相变，提出了一种由布里渊区内特定狄拉克点定义的局域拓扑标记，并关联到ARPES可测量的谱函数，同时展示了其对温度的鲁棒性及物理响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 $p_x + ip_y$ 超导体在方格上的拓扑相变，尤其是半填充情形，并寻找可实验观测的拓扑不变量，以连接理论拓扑性质与实际测量手段。

Method: 通过引入在Bloch球上定义的两个一维 $\mathbb{Z}$（$\mathbb{Z}_2$）不变量，构造了基于布里渊区中特定狄拉克点的局域拓扑标记。将该标记与频域积分后的电子谱函数（即局域动量分辨电子密度）联系，并考虑温度效应。通过在小动量区域内积分以模拟测量不确定度，同时保留拓扑信息。此外，分析了该标记的实空间表示（关联函数形式）并计算拓扑超流密度等物理响应。

Result: 局域拓扑标记即使包括温度效应仍保持良好保护；在小区域积分后可揭示态密度的Van Hove对数轮廓（体现在局域标记的导数中），同时不丢失拓扑信息。拓扑转变对应受保护的半金属态，并得到了拓扑超流密度等物理响应。

Conclusion: 该局域拓扑标记为实验探测 $p_x + ip_y$ 超导体的拓扑相变提供了可行方案，结合ARPES测量和动量空间积分可有效捕捉拓扑特征，且对温度鲁棒，为理解相关超导系统提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We present an analysis on the geometrical and physical nature of the $p_x + ip_y$ superconductor on the square lattice, with an emphasis on the topological phase transition at half-filling. We develop a local topological marker from specific Dirac points within the Brillouin zone, which is introduced via the addition of two one-dimensional (1D) $\mathbb{Z}$ $(\mathbb{Z}_2$) invariants defined on the Bloch sphere. We relate this topological marker to the electron spectral function integrated on frequency through the local momentum-resolved electron density, which may be measured via Angle Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES), and show that it remains well-protected including temperature effects. Integrating on a small area around a specific point in momentum space associated to the measure uncertainty, this also reveals the Van Hove logarithmic profile of the density of states in the derivative of the local marker while preserving the topological information. Topological transitions correspond to a protected semi-metal. We analyse the real space representation of this topological marker from correlation functions. We present physical responses such as the topological superfluid density.

</details>


### [104] [Spin Polarization of a Two-Dimensional Electron Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09533)
*G. A. Nikolaev,M. E. Sergeev,I. V. Kukushkin,A. V. Shchepetilnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 实验表明，强关联二维电子体系的纵向电阻可直接定量反映其自旋极化；通过电探测电子自旋共振独立标定自旋态，建立了磁输运与自旋极化之间的无参数关系，仅凭输运测量即可重构自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种直接、定量的手段来测量强关联二维电子体系的自旋极化，以便研究其磁态；传统方法可能依赖复杂标定或不能单独由输运给出极化信息。

Method: 实验测量纵向电阻，并利用电探测电子自旋共振作为自旋态的独立校准，建立纵向电阻与自旋极化之间的参数无关关系；随后从输运数据重构自旋极化，并用其解释共振信号及相图。

Result: 纵向电阻定量给出自旋极化；重构的自旋极化能够定量解释电探测自旋共振信号随磁场和温度的变化，并在宽载流子密度和磁场范围内描绘电子液体的磁态。

Conclusion: 纵向磁输运可作为强关联二维电子体系中自旋极化的直接探针，为从输运测量重建电子液体的磁态提供了一种简洁而普适的方法。

Abstract: We show experimentally that the longitudinal resistance of a strongly correlated two-dimensional electron system provides a direct quantitative measure of its spin polarization. Using electrically detected electron spin resonance as an independent calibration of the spin state, we establish a parameter-free relation between magnetotransport and spin polarization, enabling spin polarization to be reconstructed from transport measurements alone. The extracted spin polarization quantitatively explains the magnetic field and temperature dependence of the electrically detected spin-resonance signal and allows the magnetic state of the electron liquid to be mapped over a broad range of carrier densities and magnetic fields.

</details>


### [105] [Uniaxial stress effects on magnetic and electric properties of the ground state in a centrosymmetric magnetic skyrmion host Gd$_2$PdSi$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09680)
*Hiraku Saito,Naoki Kobayashi,Takuro Kawasaki,Tatsuya Nakamura,Akiko Kikkawa,Yasujiro Taguchi,Yoshinori Tokura,Taro Nakajima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对中心对称磁性斯格明子化合物Gd₂PdSi₃施加垂直c轴的单轴压缩应力，结合磁化、电阻率与中子衍射测量，发现其基态并非单-q磁序，而是多-q磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该化合物在场诱导第一相中具有三重-q斯格明子晶格和巨大拓扑霍尔效应，但其基态磁结构究竟是单-q还是多-q尚不清楚，需要直接检验。

Method: 在垂直c轴方向施加单轴压缩应力，同时进行磁化、电阻率及中子衍射实验，观察是否出现单-q磁序所预期的各向异性响应。

Result: 实验数据未展现出单-q磁序应有的各向异性磁电性质，排除了单-q基态，表明基态为多-q磁有序。

Conclusion: Gd₂PdSi₃的基态磁结构属于多-q磁序，这为理解该体系中斯格明子相的出现及其与基态的关系提供了关键微观基础。

Abstract: We investigate effects of uniaxial stress to magnetic orders and electrical resistivity of the centrosymmetric magnetic skyrmion compound Gd$_2$PdSi$_3$, which has a hexagonal crystal structure composed of triangular lattice layers of magnetic Gd$^{3+}$ ions. This compound is known to exhibit the triple-$q$ magnetic skyrmion lattice phase with a giant topological Hall effect in the first field induced phase [T. Kurumaji $\textit{et al}$. Science $\textbf{365}$, 914-918 (2019)]. In contrast to the established picture of the field-induced phase, the ground state of this system still remains to be studied. Although previous studies reported the existence of the incommensurate magnetic modulations described by a magnetic modulation wave vector ${\bf q}=(q,0,0)$ where $q\sim 0.14$ and its equivalents, it is still unclear whether the magnetic structure is a single-$q$ structure or a multiple-$q$ structure. In the present study, we performed magnetization, resistivity and neutron diffraction measurements with a compressive uniaxial stress applied perpendicular to the $c$ axis. The observed data revealed that the system did not exhibit anisotropic magnetic and electric properties expected from a single-$q$ magnetic order, suggesting that the magnetic ground state of this system is a multi-$q$ magnetic order.

</details>


### [106] [Robust spin pseudogap and spin-charge separation in the $σt$-$J$ model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09689)
*Zheng-Yuan Yue,Jia-Xin Zhang,Zheng-Cheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了σt-J模型，并通过iPEPS和TNR数值方法研究其有限温度性质，发现该模型在掺杂下仍保持稳健的自旋赝能隙和BKT转变，与t-J模型及其易平面变体形成对比，并用slave-fermion平均场理论解释了该稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统t-J模型中掺杂空穴与自旋背景之间存在强烈的相位弦效应干扰，为了消除这种干扰，研究者提出了σt-J模型，即翻转自旋向下跳跃项的符号。本文旨在探究该模型在有限温度下的自旋热力学和掺杂行为，并与t-J模型及易平面变体比较，以理解自旋赝能隙的起源和掺杂依赖性。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）算法计算有限温度性质，使用张量网络重整化（TNR）方法判定BKT转变；同时基于数值得到的跳跃和配对关联，通过投影对称群（PSG）约束构建slave-fermion平均场理论。

Result: σt-J模型与t-J模型及其易平面变体具有相似的自旋赝能隙热力学：自旋磁化率在T*~J处存在峰值，比热呈现宽峰。由于σt-J模型具有约化的U(1)自旋旋转对称性，其自旋扇区在较低温度T_BKT<T*处发生BKT转变。但与t-J模型及易平面变体不同，σt-J模型在掺杂后这些特征仍然稳健：掺杂空穴保持甚至增强xy自旋面内的反铁磁关联，使T*和T_BKT几乎不随掺杂变化。

Conclusion: σt-J模型通过消除相位弦效应，展现了掺杂下稳健的自旋赝能隙和BKT转变，说明自旋赝能隙的鲁棒性可能源于缺乏相位弦干扰。slave-fermion平均场理论能够解释这一掺杂稳健的物理行为，为理解高温超导体中的赝能隙现象提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The $σt$-$J$ model, obtained from the usual $t$-$J$ model by flipping the sign of the spin-down hopping term, has been proposed to eliminate the strong interference between doped holes and the spin background, known as the phase string effect. In this work, we investigate the finite-temperature properties of the $σt$-$J$ model using infinite projected entangled-pair state (iPEPS) algorithms, in comparison with the $t$-$J$ model and its easy-plane variants. We show that these models share similar spin-pseudogap thermodynamics, with a maximum in the spin susceptibility at $T^* \sim J$ and a broad specific-heat peak. Tensor network renormalization (TNR) further provides strong evidences for a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition in the spin sector of the $σt$-$J$ model at a lower temperature $T_\text{BKT} < T^*$ due to its reduced $\mathrm{U}(1)$ spin-rotation symmetry. Crucially, in contrast to the $t$-$J$ model and to its easy-plane variants that also exhibit BKT transitions, these signatures remain robust upon doping: the doped holes preserve and even enhance the antiferromagnetic correlations in the $xy$ spin plane, leading to a weak doping dependence of both $T^*$ and $T_\text{BKT}$. Finally, we develop a slave-fermion mean-field theory for the $σt$-$J$ model, whose projective symmetry group (PSG) is selected based on numerically determined hopping and pairing correlations, and show that it explains the robust spin pseudogap upon doping.

</details>


### [107] [Anomalous temperature dependence of polaron mobility in a nonlinear double-well potential: unbiased X-propagator approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09883)
*Stefano Ragni,Osor S. Barišić,Naoto Nagaosa,Andrey S. Mishchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出无偏X传播子方法，计算任意非线性电子-声子相互作用下的有限温度光导率和直流迁移率，并用于双阱晶格势的极化子模型，揭示强非谐软模导致的异常输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性耦合极化子模型无法解释某些极性金属中的反常输运现象，如SrTiO3中稀薄极性金属表现出的凹形迁移率温度依赖性和Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限的违反。该工作旨在探讨非线性电子-声子耦合是否可作为这些异常现象的微观机制。

Method: 发展了无偏的X-propagator方法，可在有限温度下计算光学电导率σ(ω)和直流迁移率μ，适用于任意非线性电子-声子相互作用。将该方法应用于与双阱晶格势耦合的极化子，模拟强非谐极性材料。

Result: 中等耦合下，迁移率呈现三个温度区间：阱内局域、热涨落越过势垒、以及势垒无关的高温动力学，产生传统线性耦合模型所没有的凹形温度依赖。强耦合下迁移率变为非单调，且光学谱权重发生特征性再分布。对SrTiO3相关参数，定性再现了反常凹形迁移率和Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限违反的起始行为。

Conclusion: 非线性耦合到软非谐晶格模式可能是稀薄极性金属中异常输运的微观机制，结果支持这一理论解释，并展示了所提方法在强非谐电子-声子体系中的有效性。

Abstract: We develop an unbiased X-propagator method for calculating finite-temperature optical conductivity $σ(ω)$ and dc mobility $μ$ for arbitrary nonlinear electron-phonon interaction. We apply it to a polaron coupled to a double-well lattice potential, a minimal model for strongly anharmonic polar materials. At moderate coupling, the mobility exhibits three temperature regimes associated with confinement within one well, thermal competition with the barrier, and barrier-insensitive high-temperature dynamics. This sequence produces a concave temperature dependence of the mobility that is absent in conventional linear-coupling polaron models. At strong coupling, the mobility becomes nonmonotonic, and its temperature evolution is reflected in a characteristic redistribution of optical spectral weight. For parameters relevant to SrTiO$_3$, our results qualitatively reproduce both the anomalous concave mobility and the onset of violation of the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, thereby supporting nonlinear coupling to a soft anharmonic lattice mode as a microscopic mechanism for anomalous transport in dilute polar metals.

</details>


### [108] [Tunable Multiband Geometry and Fractional Phases in Higher Vortexable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09911)
*Xiaohan Wan,Siddhartha Sarkar,Ting Cao,Mark Rudner,Di Xiao,Kai Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出高涡旋度莫尔带不仅可用于实现拓扑平带，还提供了一种可调多带平台：在能带色散、简并度和拓扑不变的前提下，通过连续改变波函数几何来驱动多体量子相变；精确对角化显示零磁场下出现从阿贝尔到非阿贝尔的级联拓扑相，且带间混合会移动最优量子几何区域但不破坏非阿贝尔拓扑序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点聚焦于高涡旋度对构造类似高阶朗道能级的量子几何与拓扑平带的作用，但缺乏对波函数几何本身作为独立调控参数的关注；同时多数研究仅投影到单带，忽略了完整平带希尔伯特空间中的带间混合效应对多体相的影响。

Method: 对分数填充的高涡旋度莫尔系统进行系统精确对角化，保留完整平带希尔伯特空间而非投影到单带；在固定填充下，仅改变波函数几何并考察相互作用多体基态，同时采用屏蔽库仑相互作用并系统分析各相之间的转变或交叉。

Result: 零磁场下发现包括整数与分数激子绝缘体、阿贝尔分数量子反常霍尔态、Moore-Read态和Read-Rezayi态在内的级联多体相；相同填充下，仅改变波函数几何即可在竞争拓扑态之间驱动转变；在对应Moore-Read和Read-Rezayi的填充下，带间混合会改变最优量子几何区域，但不会压制非阿贝尔拓扑序。

Conclusion: 高涡旋度莫尔带是一种零磁场下可调的多带拓扑平台，量子几何本身可作为直接调控参数，用于探索几何驱动的多带拓扑物相与非阿贝尔态。

Abstract: Higher vortexability is often viewed as a route to topological flat bands with higher-Landau-level-like quantum geometry. Here we emphasize a complementary perspective: it provides a tunable multiband structure in which wave function geometry can be varied continuously while the band dispersion, degeneracy, and topology remain fixed. We perform systematic exact-diagonalization studies of many-body phases in fractionally filled higher vortexable moiré systems, retaining the full flat-band Hilbert space rather than projecting onto a single band. The multiband treatment reveals a cascade of Abelian and non-Abelian phases at zero magnetic field, including integer and fractional exciton insulators, Abelian fractional Chern insulators, Moore-Read and Read-Rezayi states. At fixed filling, different phases are connected through transitions or crossovers driven solely by changes in wave function geometry, highlighting quantum geometry itself as a direct tuning parameter between competing topological states. At fillings associated with Moore-Read and Read-Rezayi states, our calculations show that interband mixing shifts the optimal quantum geometry regime without suppressing non-Abelian topological order under screened Coulomb interaction. Our results establish higher vortexable moiré bands as a tunable platform for exploring geometry-driven multiband topological phases at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [109] [Cell Natural Orbitals in Interacting Topological Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09932)
*Nishchhal Verma,Harshitra Mahalingam,Daniel Muñoz-Segovia,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出以胞自然轨道（CNO）为基础的奇异值分解框架，用于表征拓扑能带的投影态密度矩阵与形状因子，从而按量子几何分层截断希尔伯特空间，并以最少的局域轨道复现短程相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑能带具有无法同时满足指数局域化与对称性的Wannier函数，破坏了用有限范围局域轨道表示投影相互作用的传统范式；需要一种能忠实保留形状因子与量子几何的系统化截断方案。

Method: 对能带投影密度形状因子进行奇异值分解，将单位胞约化单粒子密度矩阵的本征态定义为胞自然轨道（CNO），其占据数刻画表示波函数重叠所需的最小轨道复杂度；据此按CNO通道对相互作用强度分层，并对弱通道采用静态平均场处理、强通道保留动态自能。

Result: 在手性极限的魔角扭曲双层石墨烯中，主导CNO位于AA位点，类似重费米子模型中的f费米子；次主导通道耦合强度递减。该方法还揭示胞内电荷密度变化导致CNO包络函数的动量依赖，进而产生单粒子谱函数色散。

Conclusion: CNO为能带拓扑与实空间关联之间提供了几何感知的桥梁，可作为分析量子材料中相互作用与涌现相的系统框架。

Abstract: Topological bands exhibit obstruction to exponentially localized and symmetric Wannier functions, challenging the standard paradigm of representing projected interactions in terms of local orbitals with finite range. To faithfully capture the form factors and quantum geometry of topological bands we introduce a singular-value decomposition of the band-projected density form factors, enabling a geometry-based truncation scheme of the Hilbert space, exposing an intrinsic hierarchy on band-projected interactions that is determined by the underlying wavefunctions. This decomposition is most naturally described in terms of Cell Natural Orbitals (CNOs), as the eigenstates of the unit-cell reduced one-particle density matrix, whose occupation provide a measure of the minimal orbital complexity required to faithfully represent the band wavefunctions overlaps. The CNO decomposition identifies systematically the minimal number of local orbitals needed to reproduce short-ranged interactions while resolving the hierarchy of interaction strengths across CNO channels. Applied to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene in the chiral limit, we find that the dominant CNO is centered at the AA site, resembling the $f$-fermion of the heavy-fermion model. The subdominant CNO channels carry progressively weaker interaction matrix elements, allowing them to be treated at the static mean-field level, while the dominant channel requires a dynamical self-energy. The formalism illustrates how variations of charge density within the unit cell generate momentum dependence in the CNO envelope function and, consequently, dispersion in the single-particle spectral function. More broadly, our results establish CNOs as a geometry-informed bridge between band topology and real-space correlations, providing a systematic framework for analyzing interactions and emergent phases in quantum materials.

</details>
